THE PLUGIN CONTRACT\n\n```ts\n// ============================================================================\n// src/core/plugin.ts\n//\n// GOVERNING INVARIANT (Design 1, adopted verbatim):\n//   No Phase A commit changes _verify_digest.js AND index.html/src together.\n//   The moment the oracle and the app move in one commit, \"60/60 green\" stops\n//   being evidence and becomes a tautology.\n//   Corollary (the refinement that makes A4 legal): an ORACLE-ONLY commit — zero\n//   app diff, proven identical verdicts on the frozen baseline — does not violate\n//   this. Co-movement is what is forbidden, not touching the file.\n//\n// WHY AN ADAPTER AND NOT A NEW eval SIGNATURE:\n//   _verify_digest.js:189 calls `def.eval(nd.params, ins, nd)` and asserts\n//   ArrayBuffer.isView(primary) (:350) and primary.length === RES*RES (:354).\n//   Failing either SKIPS the node, which the gate reports as `missing`, which FAILS.\n//   Returning {height, aux} would skip 14 types and turn 60/60 into 46/60 — a\n//   failure that reads as an absence. definePlugin therefore PROJECTS the legacy\n//   shape; the modern contract is the authoring surface only.\n// ============================================================================\n\ntype NodeTypeId = string\ntype Field      = Float32Array\ntype Affine     = [number, number, number, number, number, number] | null\n\n/* ─── ch27 Standard Map Registry — WIRE NAMES. ch27 §Naming: the tool never\n       re-names per engine. The three unions encode ch27's state/derived split at\n       the type level, because the two have OPPOSITE lifecycles.               */\ntype StateMap   = 'height' | 'soilDepth' | 'sedimentDepth' | 'sandDepth'\n                | 'snowDepth' | 'wetness' | 'moisture' | 'waterDepth'\n                | 'strataHardness' | 'temperature'\ntype VectorMap  = 'windVector' | 'flowVelocity'\ntype DerivedMap = 'curvature' | 'ao' | 'slope' | 'aspect' | 'insolation'\n                | 'twi' | 'flowAccum' | 'waterSurface'\ntype PreviewMap = 'albedo' | 'solarShadow'\ntype MapName    = StateMap | VectorMap | DerivedMap | PreviewMap\n\n/* ch27 §State vs derived, verbatim: \"Derived maps are pure functions of the final\n   fields: curvature, ao, slope, aspect, insolation, TWI.\" Design 1 filed\n   `insolation` under state and called it a match — that attaches state lifecycle\n   (carried through, never recomputed) to a map ch27 requires be recomputed after\n   the last height write. This set is asserted at registration so the error cannot\n   be re-made by a plugin author. */\nconst DERIVED_BY_DEFINITION = new Set<MapName>(\n  ['curvature', 'ao', 'slope', 'aspect', 'insolation', 'twi'])\n\n/* ch27 requires the wetness HYBRID be split explicitly — \"the state map is\n   evidence, the derived map is a prediction; do not average them in the tool and\n   lose both meanings.\" All three designs shipped one `wetness`. Hence `wetness`\n   (state, path-dependent, written by hydraulic/glacial/snow) AND `twi` (derived,\n   recomputed from final height). A plugin declaring one does not satisfy the other. */\n\ntype Unit = 'm' | 'm2' | 'm3' | 'm/s' | 'degC' | 'degC/km' | 'deg' | 'tan'\n          | 'mm/yr' | '1/yr' | '1/m' | 'ratio' | 'norm01' | 'cells' | 'none'\n\n/* ─── Phase A legacy bridge. The oracle reads aux by DOTTED PATH off the node\n       object (reach(), _verify_digest.js:276). Preserving that path is what lets a\n       declared multi-map output contract land with the baseline untouched.      */\ntype LegacyBind =\n  | { slot: `_${string}` }                 // assign the returned Field verbatim\n  | { slot: `_${string}`; opaque: true }   // returned value is the LEGACY STRUCT,\n                                           // not yet a Field of `channels` width\n  | { none: true }                         // a new port with no legacy counterpart\n\n/* `opaque` is the honest name for the two ports whose legacy slot is an object:\n     _wind       {u,v,speed,...}   — d_wind:4487, d_windmodify:4505\n     _snowLayer  {depthM, volumeM3, ...} — snow:4594\n   Declaring channels:2 while shipping a 3-array object would be a lie in the type.\n   `opaque:true` says \"this is the legacy struct; Phase B decomposes it\", and\n   _verify_manifest.js counts opaque ports so the number can only go down.       */\n\ninterface OutPort {\n  map: MapName\n  kind: 'state' | 'derived' | 'preview'\n  unit: Unit\n  channels: 1 | 2 | 3\n  /* ch27 §Vector encoding: windVector/flowVelocity are world-space (u,v) m/s,\n     +X east +Y north, never normalised, never hue-encoded, never lattice-snapped. */\n  encoding?: 'linear' | { kind: 'temperature'; minC: number; maxC: number }\n  /* index.html:3202 — TEMP_MIN_C=-100, TEMP_MAX_C=1400, packed into [0,1] with the\n     meaning carried in a FIELD_META WeakMap sidecar. Promote sidecar -> port. */\n  derivedFrom?: MapName[]\n  /* REQUIRED when kind==='derived'. Feeds ch27's manifest parent-hash rule:\n     \"an engine that loads curvature whose parent hash does not match height's is\n     loading a lie.\" The runtime stamps the parent cache key onto the port. */\n  legacy: LegacyBind\n}\n\n/* snow returns SEVEN float64 scalars the oracle pins individually\n   (_verify_digest.js:270-275: volumeM3 depositedM3 meltedM3 movedM3 maxDepthM\n   coverage iterationsRun). An aux model admitting only Float32Array forces snow\n   back into a node mutation and re-opens the hole this contract exists to close. */\ninterface ScalarPort { name: string; unit: Unit; legacy: LegacyBind }\n\n/* ch27 §Verification. This is literally today's droplet ledger\n   (index.html:3906) generalised, plus ch27's refinement: sum the STACK, not the\n   layers separately, because layers legitimately exchange mass. `derived` is\n   required because the GPU pipe path (index.html:1495) infers `exported` by\n   elimination and writes exportedDerived:true — _verify_erosion_mass.js G3\n   already depends on knowing that. */\ninterface MassBudget {\n  process: ProcessId\n  stack: MapName[]\n  sumIn: number; sumOut: number\n  settled: number; exported: number; lost: number; gain: number\n  derived?: boolean\n  named?: Record<string, number>\n}\n\ninterface Outputs {\n  height?: Field | null\n  maps?: Partial<Record<MapName, Field | unknown>>   // unknown only for opaque ports\n  scalars?: Record<string, number>\n  color?: Field | null                               // RGB, 3*len\n  budget?: MassBudget[]\n  error?: string | null                              // -> nd._inputError\n}\n\n/* ─── ch27's node table as DATA, so co-evolution is checked at registration\n       rather than reviewed by eye.                                            */\ntype ProcessId = 'hydraulic' | 'thermal' | 'aeolian' | 'glacial'\n               | 'soil-production' | 'orographic' | 'snow'\n\nconst CO_EVOLUTION: Record<ProcessId, MapName[]> = {\n  hydraulic:         ['sedimentDepth', 'soilDepth', 'wetness', 'flowVelocity', 'waterDepth'],\n  thermal:           ['soilDepth'],           // talus is loose debris; it ADDS to the cover\n  aeolian:           ['sandDepth', 'moisture'],\n  glacial:           ['soilDepth', 'wetness', 'flowVelocity'],\n  'soil-production': ['soilDepth'],\n  orographic:        ['moisture'],\n  snow:              ['snowDepth', 'wetness', 'soilDepth'],\n}\n/* NAMING TRAP, from Design 1, and it is live: this app's `weathering` node\n   (index.html:4626) is a COLOUR effect that paints rock stains — NOT ch27's\n   weathering / Heimsath soil-production process. It declares process: undefined\n   and outPorts [{map:'albedo', kind:'preview'}]. Design 3's A8 instructs the\n   opposite and would demand soilDepth from a node that tints pixels. The process\n   id is therefore spelled 'soil-production', never 'weathering', so the app's node\n   name cannot be mistaken for the process. */\n\ninterface ParamDesc {\n  key: string                    // HASHED — mk() keys node params by it\n  label: string                  // free to change; never hashed\n  type: 'slider' | 'number' | 'select' | 'seg' | 'tabs' | 'text' | 'curve'\n      | 'seed' | 'layerStack' | 'strokes'\n  def: unknown                   // HASHED — changing any default moves that digest\n  min?: number; max?: number; step?: number\n  scale?: 'log'; floor?: number; zero?: boolean\n  opts?: Array<[string, string]> | null   // null = live registry (satmap:4600 -> SATMAPS:985)\n  fmt?: (v: any) => string       // 63 inline closures, 13 closing over terrainDef. CODE.\n  when?: { key: string; values: string[] }\n  group?: string\n  // ---- all additive, all OPTIONAL, nothing reads them in Phase A ----\n  unit?: Unit                    // ch14:83-87. `unit:'cells'` forces resolutionPolicy bound.\n  invalidates?: 'VALUE' | 'TOPOLOGY' | 'COMPOSE_ONLY'\n  quantise?: number\n  promotableToField?: boolean\n}\n/* `unit` and `invalidates` are OPTIONAL, not mandatory. Design 2 made both\n   required, which forces ~470 judgement calls across 235 declarations in one\n   commit for fields nothing reads. Getting them wrong would be silent. Optional\n   + a gate that counts declared coverage upward is the same destination without\n   the one-shot risk. */\n\ninterface PluginSpec {\n  typeId: NodeTypeId             // the TYPES key. FROZEN — the digest baseline and\n                                 // every saved graph key off it. NOT versioned in\n                                 // Phase A (ch14:23's \"erosion.pipe/2\" is a rename).\n  cat: 'gen'|'comb'|'filt'|'ero'|'mask'|'data'|'effect'|'out'\n  name: string\n  desc: string\n  note?: string\n  info?: (nd: NodeInstance) => string   // transform only (:4270); reads nd._xfMode\n\n  /* ---- ports ---- */\n  ins: string[]\n  /* ORDER IS A HASHED QUANTITY. The oracle wires a DISTINCT perlin seed per slot\n     (SEEDS={A:101,B:202,C:303,M:404,D:505}, WIRING table at :225-254) precisely so\n     a slot swap moves the digest. NEVER reorder. NEVER append an aux map here —\n     that changes nodeInputs(nd).length and moves the digest for zero behavioural\n     reason. Aux travels on the side channel, ctx.inputAux(slot, map). This is the\n     single sharpest constraint the gate imposes on the shape of the contract, and\n     it is why Design 2's \"declare an inPort of kind soilDepth\" model is rejected:\n     it would cost a --write re-baseline plus a saved-graph edge.slot migration for\n     every erosion node that gained a map. */\n  variadic?: { min: number; max: number; label: string; layerParam?: string }\n  /* colormixer only. Its ports are INSTANCE state (makeNode:4722 assigns\n     nd._inputs, nodeInputs:4050 prefers it over TYPES[t].ins), so `ins` is\n     genuinely not a type-level property for it. Declared, not special-cased. */\n  referenceOnly?: number[]\n  outPorts: OutPort[]\n  scalarsOut?: ScalarPort[]\n  readsAux?: MapName[]\n  process?: ProcessId\n  coEvolutionDebt?: Partial<Record<MapName, string>>\n  /* SEPARATE from outPorts, deliberately. Design 3 put the escape INSIDE `writes`\n     (\"marked implemented:false\"), which satisfies its own boot assertion for free\n     and reduces the wall to decoration. Debt is a distinct map of\n     port -> WHY IT IS UNMET, and _verify_coevolution.js diffs it against a frozen\n     baseline: it may SHRINK, never GROW. */\n\n  /* ---- semantics ---- */\n  fieldSemantics?: 'preserve-primary' | 'temperature-pair'\n  /* 5 nodes (warp:4205 blur:4231 transform:4245 / blend:4173 maxmin:4179), read by\n     propagateFieldMetadata:3229-3231, which the ORACLE itself calls at :190.\n     Default is DROP and the default is deliberate (comment :3222-3224) — a\n     migration defaulting to 'preserve' changes 55 node types at once. */\n  effect?: 'water'|'snow'|'satmap'|'colorerosion'|'weathering'|'satmapblend'|'colormixer'\n  heightPassthrough?: boolean    // markParamDirty:4781 — 7 types\n  colorPassthrough?: boolean     // evaluators :4848 and :4883 — 2 types (water, snow)\n  /* TWO facets of one concept that disagree BY CONSTRUCTION, both load-bearing for\n     what gets rebuilt on a drag. Phase A carries both and UNIFIES NEITHER: the\n     oracle always evaluates cold (_dirty:true on every node, :156), so a wrong\n     unification passes 60/60 and ships. */\n  exactUnderTransform?: boolean  // replaces EXACT_TYPES — 19 members, not 18\n  reentrant?: boolean            // transform only; grants ctx.evaluateUpstream\n  gpuVariant?: 'square-only'     // gpuReady() = USE_GPU && lattice!==\"hex\" && GPU.init() (:1502)\n\n  /* ---- ch14 flags, dead data in Phase A ---- */\n  locality: 'local' | { neighbourhood: { metres: number } } | 'global'\n  resolutionPolicy: 'invariant' | { bound: 'cells' } | { bound: 'iterations' }\n  scalingPolicy?: (p: any, ctx: EvalContext) => any\n  determinism: 'deterministic' | { stochastic: { seedParam: string } }\n  device: 'cpu' | 'gpu' | 'either'\n  serialisable: boolean          // false = \"not preset-serialisable yet\"; a gate counts it down\n\n  params: ParamDesc[]\n  compute(ctx: EvalContext): Outputs\n  color?(ctx: ColorContext): Field | null\n}\n```\n\n### EvalContext\n\n```ts\ninterface EvalContext {\n  /* Design 3's EvalContext had NO params member — the signature all 60 plugins\n     are written against could not read its own parameters, while its own\n     scalingPolicy referenced a `Params` type the entry point never received. */\n  readonly params: Readonly<Record<string, any>>\n  readonly node: NodeInstance\n  readonly typeId: NodeTypeId\n\n  /* ---- inputs are INDEX-KEYED, never label-keyed ----\n     Design 1 bound ports by label (`ports[spec.ins[0]]`) and asserted only order\n     and length, never uniqueness — and it breaks outright on colormixer, whose\n     nd._inputs grows to 15 at runtime. Index-keying makes the variadic node fall\n     out for free and removes an unstated invariant. */\n  readonly inputCount: number                          // === ins.length, live\n  input(slot: number): Field | null\n  inputAux(slot: number, map: MapName): Field | unknown | null\n  inputMeta(slot: number): FieldMeta | null            // FIELD_META, index.html:3202\n\n  /* ---- the 8 hidden inputs, as LIVE GETTERS over the existing bindings ---- */\n  readonly res: number          // RES              index.html:972   (mutable, written at :2611)\n  readonly rows: number         // fieldH()         :1066\n  readonly len: number          // fieldLen()       :1067\n  readonly lattice: 'square' | 'hex'                 // terrainDef.lattice :6134\n  readonly cellSizeM: number    // terrainDef.scale/RES :6135\n  readonly resScale: number     // SCALE_RES?RES/REF_RES:1 :1221 (18 call sites)\n  readonly quality: 'interactive' | 'final'          // BUILD_QUALITY :1222 (14 reads)\n  readonly xf: Affine           // XF               :1109 (mutable, written at :4263)\n  readonly world: Readonly<TerrainDef>               // terrainDef :6133 — LIVE, never a spread\n\n  readonly L: LatticeOps\n  newField(v?: number): Field   // NULLARY. 104 call sites, length-sensitive digest.\n  hash(x: number, y: number, seed: number): number   // index.html:1128, Math.imul, GLSL-parity\n\n  withResolution<T>(w: number, fn: () => T): T       // wraps atFeatureScale :2610-2611\n  withTransform<T>(xf: Affine, fn: () => T): T       // wraps transform.eval :4263-4264\n\n  /* granted ONLY to plugins declaring the matching capability */\n  canEvaluateExact?(slot: number): boolean           // exactChain :4051-4056\n  evaluateUpstream?(slot: number, o?: { exact?: boolean }): Field | null   // evalExact :4059\n  gpu?(): GpuBackend | null\n\n  ledger(b: MassBudget): void\n  readonly cancelled: boolean   // buildRun generation counter, :4870\n}\n\ninterface LatticeOps {          // pure; imports nothing that reads document state\n  readonly rowPitch: number     // isHex()?HEX_ROW:1  — the idiom is inlined 10×\n  parityShift(y: number): number// hex?0.5*(y&1):0    — inlined 8×\n  neighbours(y: number): ReadonlyArray<[number, number]>   // hexNb :6174, D6 odd-r one-ring\n  readonly gradientBasis: { ex: number[]; ey: number[] }   // HEX_EX/HEX_EY :6178, Σeₖeₖᵀ=3I\n  rowsFor(w: number): number    // latticeRows :1064\n  sampleBilinear(f: Field, x: number, y: number): number   // :1069-1090\n  domainUV(x: number, y: number): [number, number]         // authored space\n  worldXY(x: number, y: number): [number, number]          // metres\n}\n```\n\n**Why the context is a live view, not a snapshot.** Two of the four mutable hidden inputs are **written during evaluation**: `RES` by `atFeatureScale` (`index.html:2611`, verified — `const prev=RES;let out; RES=w;try{out=fn(small);}finally{RES=prev;}   // the kernels read the global RES`) and `XF` by transform's own eval (`:4263-4264`). A frozen `{res: RES}` makes every node reached through `atFeatureScale` run at full RES instead of RES/k.\n\nThis is not hypothetical at the gate's own resolution: `erosion2`'s `erosionScale` default is `.38` (verified at `:4383`), so `featureScale = lerp(1,7,.38) = 3.28`, which clears `atFeatureScale`'s `if(!(k>1.02))` guard, and RES is rebound to `Math.round(256/3.28) = 78` inside the digest run. Getters over the same binding mean a read through `ctx` and a read of the global are the same read of the same storage — byte-identical by construction.\n\nIt also dissolves rather than reproduces the leak already in the tree: `simulateTerrainWind` (`:3444-3445`) accepts `def=context.terrainDef||terrainDef` and then reads the **global** `terrainDef.lattice` at `:3465`. One binding cannot disagree with itself.\n\n### `definePlugin` and the shim\n\n```ts\nexport function definePlugin(spec: PluginSpec): LegacyTypeEntry {\n  validate(spec)                                     // throws at registration\n\n  const entry: LegacyTypeEntry = {\n    cat: spec.cat, name: spec.name, ins: spec.ins, desc: spec.desc,\n    params: spec.params as any,\n    note: spec.note, info: spec.info,\n    passthrough: spec.heightPassthrough || undefined,\n    effect: spec.effect,\n    fieldSemantics: spec.fieldSemantics,\n    referenceOnly: spec.referenceOnly,\n    plugin: spec,\n    eval: null as any,\n  }\n  // PLAIN WRITABLE OWN DATA PROPERTY. Not a getter, not a Proxy, never frozen:\n  // _verify_build_progress.js:21 does `TYPES.blur.eval = (...args)=>{...}` and\n  // bridge-surface.json records TYPES among the 11 `patched` symbols. A getter or\n  // Object.freeze breaks that gate silently.\n  entry.eval = makeShim(spec)\n  return entry\n}\n\nfunction makeShim(spec: PluginSpec) {\n  return function evalShim(params: any, ins: (Field|null)[], nd: NodeInstance): Field {\n    const ctx = makeContext(spec, params, ins, nd)\n    const out = spec.compute(ctx)\n\n    assertDeclaredWereReturned(spec, out)   // THROWS: declared port not returned\n    recordUndeclared(spec, out)             // WARNS + CI-fails: returned port not declared\n    /* Design 1's assertWritesExact threw on BOTH. In a plugin-per-node repo one\n       agent's over-declaration takes the whole app down for every other agent\n       mid-extraction — a boot-time landmine that serialises the phase it is meant\n       to parallelise. Throw on missing (that is the co-evolution guarantee);\n       collect extras and fail the gate, not the browser. */\n\n    for (const p of spec.outPorts) {\n      if (!('slot' in p.legacy)) continue\n      const v = p.map === 'height' ? out.height : out.maps?.[p.map]\n      assignDotted(nd, p.legacy.slot, v ?? null)     // SAME instance. No copy.\n    }\n    for (const s of spec.scalarsOut ?? [])\n      if ('slot' in s.legacy) assignDotted(nd, s.legacy.slot, out.scalars?.[s.name])\n\n    if (out.error !== undefined) nd._inputError = out.error\n    if (out.budget) { nd._budget = out.budget; for (const b of out.budget) ctx.ledger(b) }\n\n    return (out.height ?? ins[0] ?? ctx.newField()) as Field\n  }\n}\nexport const TYPES = Object.fromEntries(PLUGINS.map(p => [p.plugin.typeId, p]))\n```\n\n**Four identity rules the shim must obey.** Each protects a measured invariant:\n\n1. `ctx.input(i)` returns `ins[i]` **by reference** — no copy, wrap, subarray or transfer. `FIELD_META` (`index.html:3202`) is a `WeakMap` keyed on the Float32Array **object**; one copy drops every °C tag and tempmask/d_heat/snow/water fall into their `_inputError` branches (`:4461, :4505, :4592-4594`). The digest catches this loudly, which is why it is worth stating.\n2. The returned height is `out.height` **itself**. Four caches key on array reference identity — `_colorErosionRoute` (`:5978,:5998`), `_weatherSignal` (`:6031,:6060`), `_biomeMaskCache` (`:6094`), and the viewport's `curField`/`curSnowLayerRef` (`:6819-6822`) — none cleared by `markDirtyFrom`/`restoreGraph`/`applyWorkingResolution`. Fresh arrays for unchanged inputs make all four miss forever: a silent 5-50× regression the digest passes cleanly. **Contract rule: identical key ⇒ identical array INSTANCE.**\n3. Aux is scattered as the same object at the same `_name`, so `reach(nd,'_wind.u')` resolves unchanged.\n4. The fallback chain reproduces `ins[0]||newField()` exactly — the idiom the 7 passthrough evals use (e.g. `satmapblend:4642`).\n\n### Co-evolution enforcement — four layers, none of them a comment\n\n```ts\nfunction validate(spec: PluginSpec): void {\n  // L1 STRUCTURAL — not a check. compute() returns ONE Outputs bundle and there is\n  //    no other emission path: EvalContext has no aux writer and no store handle.\n  //    A plugin physically cannot compute soilDepth in a later pass. That is\n  //    ch27's \"in the same pass, from the same intermediate state\" made\n  //    unrepresentable otherwise, and it is the direct fix for the failure already\n  //    in the tree: hydroMassDiag (index.html:1443) is a module-level,\n  //    last-writer-wins global written by the GPU pipes at :1495 AND the droplets\n  //    at :3906 with no node identity. Two erosion nodes in one graph and the first\n  //    ledger is destroyed. It exists ONLY because eval returns one Float32Array\n  //    and has nowhere else to put what the simulation computed.\n\n  // L2 LIFECYCLE\n  for (const p of spec.outPorts) {\n    if (DERIVED_BY_DEFINITION.has(p.map) && p.kind !== 'derived')\n      throw new Error(`${spec.typeId}: '${p.map}' is derived by definition (ch27) — declared '${p.kind}'`)\n    if (p.kind === 'derived' && !(p.derivedFrom?.length))\n      throw new Error(`${spec.typeId}: derived port '${p.map}' must declare derivedFrom`)\n    if (p.map === 'albedo' && p.kind !== 'preview')\n      throw new Error(`${spec.typeId}: albedo is preview-only (ch27 Masking Doctrine)`)\n  }\n  /* ch27's Masking Doctrine is the one prohibition the chapter calls absolute:\n     baked colour is NOT part of the runtime handoff. It survives as a preview and\n     review product (08/09) and no engine-side system may depend on it. Designs 2\n     and 3 both shipped an albedo/colour port with no such constraint. */\n\n  // L3 CO-EVOLUTION WALL — throws; the app does not boot\n  if (spec.process && spec.outPorts.some(p => p.map === 'height')) {\n    const have = new Set(spec.outPorts.map(p => p.map))\n    const debt = new Set(Object.keys(spec.coEvolutionDebt ?? {}))\n    const missing = CO_EVOLUTION[spec.process].filter(m => !have.has(m) && !debt.has(m))\n    if (missing.length) throw new Error(\n      `${spec.typeId}: process '${spec.process}' alters height but does not co-update ` +\n      `[${missing.join(', ')}]. Produce them, or declare coEvolutionDebt with a reason.`)\n  }\n  for (const [k, why] of Object.entries(spec.coEvolutionDebt ?? {}))\n    if (!why || !String(why).trim())\n      throw new Error(`${spec.typeId}: debt entry '${k}' needs a reason string`)\n\n  // L4 RESOLUTION POLICY\n  if (spec.params.some(p => p.unit === 'cells') && spec.resolutionPolicy === 'invariant')\n    throw new Error(`${spec.typeId}: a 'cells' param forces resolutionPolicy bound (ch14:85-86)`)\n}\n```\n\n**L5, the ratchet, lives in CI.** `tests/legacy/_verify_coevolution.js` reads every plugin's `coEvolutionDebt`, sums it, and diffs against a frozen `_coevolution_baseline.json`. It fails on (i) any gap absent from the baseline, (ii) any baseline entry no longer a gap. Debt may **shrink, never grow**. It passes on day one because the baseline records exactly today's measured gaps, and it requires a **negative test** — a fixture plugin declaring `process:'hydraulic'` with `outPorts:[{map:'height',…}]` and no debt **must throw**. Without the negative test the wall is decoration.\n\n**Today's measured debt** (from the code, not from ch27's prose):\n\n- *Already ch27-compliant state ports:* `temperature` (`_temperatureC` in °C, `FIELD_META` tagged), `windVector` (`_wind.{u,v,speed}` m/s), `snowDepth` (`_snowLayer.depthM` m, with a closed volume/deposited/melted/moved budget already digested as 7 scalars).\n- *Already ch27-compliant derived maps:* `d_slope, d_height, d_curvature, d_occlusion, d_flow, d_deposits, d_wear, d_texture, d_sunshadow` — recomputed from final height. **Must not be promoted to state maps.**\n- *In debt:* `hydraulic, streampower, erosion2` owe soilDepth/wetness/flowVelocity/waterDepth/sedimentDepth; `hydrofix` owes soilDepth/wetness/flowVelocity/waterDepth; `thermal` owes soilDepth; `snow` owes wetness/soilDepth.\n- *Absent from the app entirely, no producer node exists:* `moisture, soilDepth, sedimentDepth, sandDepth, wetness, flowVelocity, waterDepth, strataHardness`. **ch27's Snow Rule (`snowfall = moisture · freezeFraction(temperature)`) is therefore currently unsatisfiable by measurement, not by opinion.** See §5.\n\n---\n\n## 2. HONEST COST TABLE\n\nMeasured by extracting each of the 60 eval bodies and probing for `gpuReady(`, `resScale()`, `RES`, `BUILD_QUALITY`, `terrainDef`, `XF`, `atFeatureScale`, node mutation, and graph reflection.\n\n**31 pure moves · 29 need real work.**\n\n| Class | n | Nodes |\n|---|---|---|\n| **Pure move** — body is a pure expression over `ctx.input(i)` and params | **31** | `worley gradient shape tectonic constant blend add maxmin stampn smax smin terrace normalizen levels curve histeq sculpt clampn invert slopemask heightmask tempmask d_slope d_height d_curvature d_flow d_occlusion d_wear d_texture water output` |\n| **Blocked without a contract extension** | 2 | **`transform`** — reflects on the graph (`inputEdge/exactChain/evalExact`), mutates global `XF`, re-enters the evaluator from inside its own eval (`:4258-4269`). Needs `reentrant` + `ctx.evaluateUpstream`. **`colormixer`** — ports are instance state (`nd._inputs`, 2-15), plus `params.layers` |\n| **Colour resolved in a second graph walk** | 5 | `satmap colorerosion weathering satmapblend colormixer` — `satmapblend`'s entire eval is `nd._mask=ins[2]\\|\\|null; return ins[0]\\|\\|newField();` (`:4642`). Real work in `resolveColor:6101` |\n| **Aux is the real output** | 7 | `d_sunshadow d_temperature d_heat d_wind d_windmodify snow satmap` — the returned field is a passthrough; the digest hashes the side channel |\n| **Mutates global RES via `atFeatureScale`** | 3 | `thermal hydraulic` (direct, `:4298/:4373`) · `erosion2` (via `erosion2Field:3938`) |\n| **Reads `gpuReady()` inside eval** | 6 | `perlin simplex ridged warp thermal hydraulic` — ungated by the digest (`USE_GPU=false`, `:110`); `_verify_gpu.js` is the gate |\n| **Reads `resScale()`/`BUILD_QUALITY`** | 4 | `blur streampower d_deposits d_peaks` |\n| **Instance/external state beyond `params`** | 3 | `drawmask` (`params.strokes`) · `colormixer` (`_inputs`+`layers`) · `import` (`node._dem/_demImg/_demRaw`, calls `buildDemFromSource(node)`, `:4170`) |\n| **Content-addressed cache, mutate-on-hit** | 1 | `canyon` — `CANYON_EVOLUTION_CACHE:2088`, key at `:2130-2136`, LRU 10; hit path mutates the shared entry (`:2253-2262`) |\n| **Module-init-time default** | 1 | `mountain` — `P.curve(\"skirt\",…,mountainSkirtDefault())` at `:4118` is a real call at load, and `makeNode` **aliases** it (see §0) |\n| **Trivial node mutation** | 2 | `layout` (`_shapeCount`) · `hydrofix` (transitive erosion helper) |\n\n*Classes overlap; the 29 distinct hard nodes are the union.* **The five genuinely hardest, in order: `transform`, `colormixer`, `snow`, `hydraulic`, `import`.**\n\n---\n\n## 4. WHAT PHASE A DELIBERATELY DOES NOT DO\n\n1. **Does not change the `eval` signature.** `def.eval(params, ins, node) -> Float32Array` of length `RES*RES` stays literally true. The oracle asserts `ArrayBuffer.isView` (`:350`) and `length === RES*RES` (`:354`), and a skip becomes `missing`, which FAILS. Cost: one closure call per node evaluation, not in a hot loop.\n2. **Does not compute a single new auxiliary map.** `soilDepth, sedimentDepth, sandDepth, wetness, moisture, flowVelocity, waterDepth, strataHardness` are declared debt with reasons and stay absent. Phase A ships the *contract and the enforcement*, not the physics.\n3. **Does not unify the two dirty classifications.** `markParamDirty` (7 types) and the evaluators' `colorDirty` rule (2 types) disagree by construction. The oracle always evaluates cold, so a wrong unification ships silently.\n4. **Does not thread `ctx` through the 104 `newField()` sites.** It stays nullary. One missed site returns a short array and drifts its node silently.\n5. **Does not touch the preview pyramid.** 26 square-only `resampleField` sites vs 4 `resampleRect`; on hex, `fieldH()=round(RES·2/√3)≠RES`, cropping ~15% of every climate/wind/snow field. Never executed at 256². A28 lands the gate; the fix is Phase B, ticketed.\n6. **Does not split the 60 into per-node files.** That is Phase B, and A26's single registry module is what makes it parallelisable.\n7. **Does not introduce a `rootSeed`.** Seeds are already params (11 declarations); `ctx.hash(x,y,seed)` is the only randomness. Design 2 planned a Phase-C re-baseline of all 11 seeded nodes — unnecessary. A stateful stream would also couple output to `evalGraphProgressive`'s cancellable rAF scheduling (`:4870,:4876,:4887`).\n8. **Does not fix `makeNode`'s default aliasing** (§0) — a real bug the digest cannot see.\n9. **Does not touch React.** No inspector work. `_verify_realtime/_verify_render/_verify_satgen` drive `#pBody .field` via `input.value = x; dispatchEvent(new Event('input'))`; React's `_valueTracker` swallows that. Three verifiers break even with byte-identical DOM, and `_verify_digest.js` is unaffected — which is exactly why that commit must not cite 60/60 as its evidence.\n10. **Does not implement ch27's staleness or snow-audit verification hooks.** `derivedFrom` makes the first mechanisable later; the second needs `moisture`, which has no producer.\n\n---\n\n## 5. THE ONE DECISION I WOULD ESCALATE\n\n**Is the co-evolution debt ratchet binding or advisory?**\n\nA13 freezes `_coevolution_baseline.json` recording the six erosion/snow nodes' unmet co-updates, and `_verify_coevolution.js` enforces *shrink-only*. That is a genuine commitment, and its size is not obvious from the brief.\n\nEight ch27 registry maps have **no producer anywhere in the app** — `moisture, soilDepth, sedimentDepth, sandDepth, wetness, flowVelocity, waterDepth, strataHardness`. Satisfying the ratchet therefore does not mean \"wire up existing data\". It means building a **soil/regolith layer model** and a **moisture/precipitation model**, and rewriting five erosion solvers to co-evolve them. ch27's Snow Rule (`snowfall = moisture · freezeFraction(temperature)`) is unsatisfiable today by measurement: the `snow` node's inputs are height/temperature/wind and no moisture field exists to gate on.\n\nThat is materially larger than \"rearchitect into React + a plugin-per-node architecture\", and it is a **product** decision, not an engineering one:\n\n- **(a) Binding.** Baseline frozen, shrink-only, CI-enforced. Phase B/C cannot ship an erosion node that ignores its implied maps. Buys real ch27 compliance; commits the project to a soil + moisture subsystem before the rearchitecture is \"done\".\n- **(b) Advisory.** `_verify_coevolution.js` prints the ledger and fails **only** on the registration wall (a *new* node must declare debt with a reason) but never on the frozen baseline. Existing debt can persist indefinitely. Keeps Phase A/B scoped to the rearchitecture; risks ch27 being retrofitted late, which is the exact failure the chapter exists to prevent.\n\nI recommend **(b) for Phase A/B and (a) at the Phase C boundary**, but the ratchet's teeth are set in A13, and re-tightening later is a policy reversal that will be argued about. I would not choose this alone.\n\n**Secondary flags** (I would take these myself, noting them for visibility): the `makeNode` default-aliasing bug (§0); and A28's preview-pyramid gate being expected red on hex from the day it lands.\n\n---\n\n### Files this spec touches\n\n- `c:/repos/GitHub/skills/apps/terrain-studio/index.html` (8,112 lines; script `:928-8110`)\n- `c:/repos/GitHub/skills/apps/terrain-studio/package.json`\n- `c:/repos/GitHub/skills/apps/terrain-studio/src/core/{plugin,context,lattice-constants,document,field}.ts` *(new)*\n- `c:/repos/GitHub/skills/apps/terrain-studio/src/plugins/registry.ts` *(new)*\n- `c:/repos/GitHub/skills/apps/terrain-studio/src/testing/bridge-block.js`\n- `c:/repos/GitHub/skills/apps/terrain-studio/tests/legacy/{_verify_digest,_verify_shapescan,bridge-surface.json}`\n- `c:/repos/GitHub/skills/apps/terrain-studio/tests/legacy/{_verify_manifest,_verify_context,_verify_coevolution,_verify_preview_pyramid}.js` *(new)*\n- `c:/repos/GitHub/skills/terrain-architect/references/27-engine-data-handoff.md` *(arrives via A0 rebase)*",

I did read 14-graph-runtime.md in full and it IS authoritative and directly applicable — its NodeDesc (14:22-30) and ParamDesc (14:70-79) are close to a superset of what TYPES already has, which is the useful result.\n\nAlso note the target path: the file is `c:/repos/GitHub/skills/apps/terrain-studio/index.html`, 8,112 lines, and the TYPES table is at 4068-4651, not 4048-4631 (4048 is EXACT_TYPES, 4015 is P). The old copy at terrain-architect/studio/index.html is still present (603,669 bytes, mtime Jul 29 08:08); I read only the apps/ copy, as instructed. The digest oracle is at apps/terrain-studio/tests/legacy/_verify_digest.js (not skills/tests/legacy — that directory exists but is empty).\n\nWHY \"entangled\" AND NOT \"clean\": the registry does separate into a manifest half and a code half, but the split is not where a plugin architecture wants it. The manifest half (cat/name/ins/desc/note/effect/fieldSemantics/referenceOnly/params-as-data) is genuinely liftable node-by-node with the digest green, because nothing in it is consulted during evaluation except `ins` arity, `referenceOnly` (evalExact only), and `fieldSemantics` (post-eval tagging). The code half is entangled through four independent mechanisms, all measured above: 13 evals write aux state onto the node object; 5 of the 7 \"effect\" nodes do their real work in a second graph walk (resolveColor) the contract does not describe; 14 evals read ambient globals that are really an EvalContext; and transform re-enters the evaluator under a mutated global XF while depending on a type-id Set that lives outside the registry. Only transform is BLOCKED outright.\n\nWHY \"entangled\" AND NOT \"blocked\": nothing here requires going red. Concretely, four Phase A commits can land with 60/60 at every one, in this order: (1) publish the `globalThis.__terrain={...}` test surface — this is the ONE commit where the oracle's own page-side accessors change, and it must be first, not last; (2) add declarative-only manifest fields (structural `unit`, LOCAL/NEIGHBOURHOOD/GLOBAL, RESOLUTION_INVARIANT/RESOLUTION_BOUND + scaling policy, `invalidates: VALUE|TOPOLOGY|COMPOSE_ONLY`, and an `exactUnderTransform` flag replacing the external EXACT_TYPES Set) — nothing reads them yet, so nothing can move; (3) promote `effect` from a dead key to the real resolveColor/collectScene discriminator, replacing two hardcoded string chains — behaviour-identical; (4) change the eval return to `{primary, aux}` and have the EVALUATOR scatter aux onto the node, because the digest reaches aux by dotted path (`reach()`) and will still find nd._temperatureC / nd._wind.u / nd._snowLayer.depthM exactly where it looks. That fourth commit turns the existing 17 ad-hoc `nd._*` fields into a declared multi-map output contract — the honest version of what the brief calls the co-evolution rule — without a single byte changing.\n\nTHE REAL PHASE B COST ESTIMATE, ranked by what is not yet pure: 5 blocked-until-contract-grows (satmap, colorerosion, weathering, satmapblend, colormixer — colour resolved in a second pass); 1 blocked outright (transform — evaluator re-entrancy + global XF mutation); 2 with schema holes that make `def.params` an incomplete description of node state (colormixer's `layers` + variadic 2-15 ports, drawmask's `strokes`); 1 with external asset state (import — reads node._dem/_demImg/_demRaw and calls buildDemFromSource(node)); 7 whose real output is aux maps rather than the returned field (d_sunshadow, d_temperature, d_heat, d_wind, d_windmodify, snow, satmap); 14 that read ambient globals; 2 that mutate the global RES via atFeatureScale (thermal, hydraulic). The remaining ~36 nodes with `(p,ins)` signatures and no global reads beyond newField/maskApply are genuinely pure today and are the cheap 60% of Phase B.\n\nTWO HAZARDS FOR WHOEVER WRITES THE CONTRACT, neither of which the digest protects. First: FIELD_META is a WeakMap keyed by Float32Array OBJECT IDENTITY (index.html:3202). Any Phase A change that wraps, copies, or transfers field buffers silently drops every physical-unit tag, and tempmask/d_heat/snow/water fall into their \"Connect a Temperature field\" branches — six or more digests move at once, from a change that looks like plumbing. Second: markParamDirty's passthrough rule (7 types, index.html:4781) and the evaluator's colorDirty rule (`nd.type!==\"water\"&&nd.type!==\"snow\"`, index.html:4852 and 4886) are two inconsistent classifications of the same concept. Unifying them is invisible to the digest, because the oracle always evaluates from cold — so a wrong unification ships silently. Both need a gate other than _verify_digest.js: the WeakMap one is partly covered by _verify_snow.js / _verify_wind.js / _verify_water.js, the dirty-propagation one by _verify_realtime.js and _verify_build_progress.js.\n\n### gl-and-ui-boundary [entangled]\n- The frame loop is a self-scheduling closed imperative loop. `renderGL()` reads only module-level render state (cam, sun, renderLook, waterLook, shadeMode, wire, scene, buffers, gbuf, wTex/hTex/iTex/isTex, uTime, terrainDef, H_SCALE, RES) and re-arms itself with requestAnimationFrame. It is entered exactly once, from the tail of initGL(). It NEVER reads graph nodes/edges and never triggers evaluation.
